# Agents · T · part 15

1,048 entries, test-preflight to test-runner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **test-preflight**: Checks the test harness BEFORE a measured run — collisions (a regen, a Forge run or another suite reading what the suite reads), the plugins the report needs, uncommitted source, what changed in the harness since the last report, and which tiers the diff touches. Returns GO / WAIT / FIX with reasons, computing nothing itself. Use as the first step of /test-report, or before any long test run. ([macrae/mana-map](https://github.com/macrae/mana-map/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-preflight.md) · ★2)
- **test-preflight**: Pre-flight contradiction classifier. Runs after the implementer commits and before the test agent runs. Enumerates existing tests that reference the changed symbols and classifies each as keep / update / delete-because-AC-supersedes. Hands a structured plan to the test agent so it doesn't fight stale tests. ([dsh-a/agent-sdlc](https://github.com/dsh-a/agent-sdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-preflight.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **test-prepare-runner**: Executes a fully-resolved Muggle Test prepare plan — detects what's listening, verifies env files, fresh-installs stale deps, starts approved services, smoke-tests, and returns the readiness table. Dispatched by the muggle-test-prepare skill after all user decisions are resolved; carries the opus pin so execution never runs below its reliability floor on a cheaper session model. ([multiplex-ai/muggle-ai-works](https://github.com/multiplex-ai/muggle-ai-works/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/test-prepare-runner.md) · ★16)
- **test-pro-metrics-agent**: Testing metrics specialist responsible for coverage interpretation, defect trends, and release-quality evidence. ([awslabs/aidlc-workflows](https://github.com/awslabs/aidlc-workflows/blob/HEAD/plugins/test-pro/agents/test-pro-metrics-agent.md) · ★6.2k · 18 copies)
- **test-probe**: Runs short experiments during work — reproductions, CSP or CORS behavior, limits, timings — and reports command and output pairs. Use when a question about behavior is faster to run than to read. ([yolol312/goodboy](https://github.com/yolol312/goodboy/blob/HEAD/agents/test-probe.md))
- **test-profiler**: Measures why KiwiDesk's test run is slow and reports the mechanism with numbers. Use when a gate or test target got slow, its time is bimodal across identical runs, a test flakes only under load, or before a minor or major release to compare against the last baseline. Read-only — it runs and samples the suite, never edits. ([KiwiCanopy/KiwiDesk](https://github.com/KiwiCanopy/KiwiDesk/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-profiler.md) · ★283)
- **test-projects**: *The ready-made harnesses, what each one exercises, and which to use for a given change.* ([XenolithEngine/xenolith-engine](https://github.com/XenolithEngine/xenolith-engine/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/test-projects.md) · ★23)
- **test-proof**: How `test-prover` runs one test on its own here, and how it tells a run that tested nothing from a pass. `pnpm` is not always on `PATH` (`docs/agents/workspace.md`), so the commands run each package's own Vitest through `npx`. ([jwh3times/Nearcited](https://github.com/jwh3times/Nearcited/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/test-proof.md) · ★1)
- **test-proof**: Juror lens for whether tests prove anything. Use at the plan gate (always): for each test in tests.md, names the smallest realistic bug that breaks its AC and the assert that would catch it, and flags tests that would pass anyway. At the pr gate (always): for each test the effort added, says whether it would still pass with the code it proves left out. ([nikolajkarpic/nio](https://github.com/nikolajkarpic/nio/blob/HEAD/src/nio/agents/test-proof.md))
- **test-prophet**: Testing strategist. Plans and reviews test suites, identifies coverage gaps, and provides example tests. ([JackScammell/Parliament-Of-Chaos](https://github.com/JackScammell/Parliament-Of-Chaos/blob/HEAD/agents/test-prophet.md) · ★4)
- **test-protocol**: *What a change owes at each stage, what each stage costs (measured), which harnesses may run at once, and the runner that decides all three.* ([XenolithEngine/xenolith-engine](https://github.com/XenolithEngine/xenolith-engine/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/test-protocol.md) · ★23)
- **test-prover**: Prover for the /prune-tests skill. In its own git worktree, it applies one batch's proposed test deletions and collapses, re-points test records in its own folders, then breaks production code on purpose to prove each removal is safe, restores any test that turns out to be the only guard of real behaviour, and commits only test-side changes. Spawned by /prune-tests with isolation "worktree"; not for direct use. ([jasonshave/agent-toolkit](https://github.com/jasonshave/agent-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/jason-toolkit/agents/test-prover.md))
- **test-prover**: Proves or disproves a test-audit candidate by breaking the production code the test guards (flip a comparison, move a boundary, delete a branch, return a constant), running the test, recording whether it stayed green, and restoring the code; also proves repairs by showing the recorded mutant now dies, an alternative correct implementation still passes, or shuffled runs agree. Dispatched by test-audit (phase 2) and test-repair (step 3). Always restores the working tree. &lt;example&gt; Context: Phase 2 of a test audit, candidates recorded user: "Prove candidates T1-3, T1-7, T1-9 on billing/invoice.p… ([mdlockyer/code-quality-plugins](https://github.com/mdlockyer/code-quality-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/behavioral-testing/agents/test-prover.md))
- **test-pruner**: Finds redundant and slow tests in Fabricks. Use to rank merge or delete candidates from timing and coverage-overlap data. Proposes only; never edits or deletes tests. ([fabricks-framework/fabricks](https://github.com/fabricks-framework/fabricks/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-pruner.md) · ★10)
